# OTHERREACH systems checklist

Audited 2026-10-24 against `playtest/humgen-main` @ 6131fc64 (worktree `D:\Otherreach_Scratch\humgen_rcd8_20261023\worktree`). Read-only audit. Every status was checked in `src/`, `content/` and `tests/`; no doc's own "done" claim was trusted. DONE means a named test was found (tests were not run). Owner rulings win over docs.

Interactive version (filter by status, tick items): see https://claude.ai/artifact/E7gLnkJXbyEdoUYbfd46HA

**Status legend:** DONE = implemented and tested. PARTIAL = part built, gap stated. STUB = placeholder only. MISSING = nothing in code. DEFERRED-BY-OWNER = owner ruling defers it (cited). CONTENT-ONLY = system works, needs more content.

## Top 10 gaps to "systems complete"

1. **[MOVED TO FUTURE SYSTEMS by owner 2026-10-25]** **Crime, law and justice are missing (QST-13..34).** Missing: ownership and theft, harmable NPCs, jurisdiction, witnesses, wanted states, arrest, prison and criminal groups. Stealth witnesses, property, controlled stones and party crime links all depend on it. It is deferred since 2026-09-25 (Q2), so the owner must say whether it is in the systems-complete bar.
2. **Player techniques are thin (CMB-24/25, PRG-29..32).** Only power strike, aimed shot and long thrust exist. There is no technique web, no parry, guard-break or gap-thrust, and no requirement records. Agility and Precision do nothing yet.
3. **Building, property and housing are missing (WLD-80..93, CAN-701..706).** Only identity stubs exist in `content/pieces`, and the old building stack is unmerged on `origin/claude/m7-factions-building`. The public website promises "Build".
4. **There is no World Event Director (QST-55..58, WLD-89).** So there are no world events, Othertide, Otherstorm or Otherwake, no caravans, sieges, festivals or migrations, and no world history. Repopulation is fixed respawn timers.
5. **There is no boss and encounter framework (CMB-38..45).** Missing: phase machines, multi-part bosses, weak-point windows, arena hazards, and the canon non-combat stand-down for duty constructs. The Ring Custodian is the only boss.
6. **Gear enhancement (spec 47) is unbuilt despite the 2026-10-21 owner ruling, and crafting is flat (ITM-64..69, 50..58).** There are no enhancement slots, no essences from salvage and no enchanting skill. Crafts are instant and have no defects or tools. No station is above grade 0, so masterwork cannot happen in play.
7. **Magic grammar is minimal (CMB-104..124).** It has 3 formulas and only self and projectile targeting. There are no implements, tuning, Necromancy or Enchantment, rituals, countermagic or residue.
8. **Masteries and the skill roster are missing (PRG-23/24).** Skills hard-stop at 60 and the level cap is 5. There are 12 skills against a canon roster of 43, and athletics and unarmed were dropped.
9. **Peoples and heritage (PRG-59..66, 73..75, 87).** Only the Veth are playable, with no innate kit. The heritage trade rule and "What Was Kept" sit on unmerged branches. The creator has only 2 baked bodies, so most choices still draw as the preset.
10. **Worn armour on HumGen bodies is missing (ITM-25/26, PRG-97).** Equipped armour only shows through the stopped MetaHuman bindings. Armour weight and noise (`movement_penalty`) are never read.

Also high on the list:
- seasons and the calendar-driven sky (WLD-33/34);
- party tactics and ranged companions (CMP-15..20);
- pet roles beyond the wolf (CMP-45..57);
- HUD modes and toggles (SYS);
- save UX: thumbnails, "Saving…", damaged-save wording (SYS);
- the Field Guide (SYS-166);
- stealth information: scent, bodies, witnesses (CMB-73..83).

**Finished work not yet in humgen-main:**
- heritage (`claude/heritage-veth-2`);
- field ruins (`claude/field-ruins`, dc74d216);
- grand scale (`claude/grand-scale`, partly uncommitted in `claude_grand_scale_wt`);
- the M7 building stack;
- `claude/rcd8-engineering`, `worker/followups2` (rock-outline collision, weather following the simulation) and `worker/combat-mocap`.

Also not in HEAD: multi-region streaming and cell residency (S-21), which the Undermeasure and Otherhome need.

Merging these is wave 0.

## Build order to "systems complete"

**Systems complete** means every row in this checklist is DONE, CONTENT-ONLY or DEFERRED-BY-OWNER. The full test suite must be green on `playtest/humgen-main`. Every visible behaviour must be accepted from a real-time route video that Claude watches frame by frame; agents' "pass" claims are not forwarded. After that, only areas, quests and content remain.

**Rules for every wave**
- **One owner per file area.** Each work package (WP) owns the folders listed. Anything outside them changes only through a small seam the coordinator agrees first.
- **Save schema numbers are reserved by the coordinator.** No WP picks its own. Each WP adds its own section codec, migration step and fixture under `tests/Persistence.Tests/Fixtures` at the number it was given. WP-1C owns the shared save infrastructure (`SaveStore`, `SaveModel`, `SaveTool`).
- **Pathspec-only commits in shared checkouts**, one coherent change per commit. Before handing back, each WP rebases on `humgen-main` and runs the full suite.
- **Gates are named tests plus, for anything visible, a real-time video.** For motion, popping or sound, frame-diff the video. Screenshots are never the gate.
- **Wave order.** A wave starts only when the WPs it depends on have merged. WPs inside a wave run in parallel.

### Wave 4: late systems (schedule after wave 3, or when the owner asks)
- **4A Endgame layer:** post-cap mastery, legacy, institutions, the access model, Stage A seeds (PRG-99..118). Needs 2E.
- **4B Great Works and divine/covenant:** needs owner-authored patrons (QST-80, PRG-103).
- **4C World-event content classes:** Othertide, Otherstorm and Otherwake on the Director, world mutation, town destruction and reclaiming (QST-55..58, 81, 93..95). Needs 1H and 2G.
- **4D Deferred by the owner; reopen only by ruling:** crime, law and justice (2F, 3B arrest/capture, the 3A witness consumption, property jurisdiction in 1G, controlled stones in 3C; owner 2026-10-25); survival effects (D6), mounts and carts (D9), soul and incarnation (OD-C18), colossals, gamepad and controller parity, the MP and modding seams beyond the guardrail tests.

After wave 3 merges, each area moves to CONTENT-ONLY. The remaining work is content: areas, quests, creatures, items and lore.

## Recommended additions (not in the docs)

The owner asked for systems that are not in the documents. The coordinator supplied a starter list and each idea was checked against the audit rows. The verdicts:
- **NEW:** not specified anywhere; proposed.
- **MERGE:** already specified; the row ID shows where, so it is not duplicated.
- **CUT:** conflicts with an owner ruling or the docs.

Every NEW item needs an owner yes before it is scheduled.

| # | Idea | Verdict | Already specified? | Why | Size | Fit with canon and owner rulings |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | Appearance wardrobe ("transmog") and dyes | NEW | No. Only disguise (CRIME doc) and creator appearance (PRG-85/91) exist. | 142 HumGen garments already exist. Showing a look separately from the stats lets players wear favourite clothes over endgame armour. | M | Look is kept separate from stats, so the heritage trade rule still governs what can be equipped or traded. Dyes are crafted from world materials (ties into crafting). Mixed-era wardrobe allowed. Violet dye could be rare and read as Other-touched. |
| R2 | Mounts, beasts of burden, large rideable creatures | MERGE (deferred) | WLD-12, CMB-148, CMP-64 (TRAVEL doc; deferred by ruling D9) | MMO staple; also carries cargo for the living economy | L | Already deferred by the owner, so it is not re-proposed. When reopened, grand scale suggests large canon beasts rather than horses only. |
| R3 | Player housing and a claimed home | MERGE | WLD-80..93, CAN-701..706 (WORLD_BUILDING_AND_PROPERTY; campfire to settlement ladder in GAMEPLAY_LOOPS) | Gives a reason to settle and a place to store things. The website promises "Build". | L | Built as WP-1G and 3D. A "claimed home" is the cottage rung of the existing ladder. |
| R4 | Dynamic world events (rift storms, migrations, invasions, dragon flyover) and world bosses (giants, dragons) | MERGE | QST-55..58, WLD-89, CMP-90, CMB-46 (QUESTS_DUNGEONS doc; colossals deferred) | Makes the world feel alive while the player is offline. | L | Built in WP-1H (Director) and 4C. It is cheap, shows grand scale, and needs no colossal combat. |
| R5 | Bestiary / codex filled by encounters and lore | MERGE | CMB-99 (PARTIAL), QST-67, WLD-72 (spec 41) | Already partly live through lore. Missing: a dedicated bestiary page that fills in with kills, study and knowledge bands. | S | Extends spec 41. Knowledge is earned, never shown in full (the no-omniscience rule). |
| R6 | Titles, deeds and achievements tied to heritage and legacy | NEW (partly seeded) | One heritage title exists ("Keeper", PRG-74, on branch). No achievement system. CAN-281 mentions achievements only in passing. | Gives long-term goals and makes heritage and legacy visible. | M | Titles are shown on the nameplate and come from heritage quests, Great Works and named kills. Local only, no online achievements (no-internet rule). |
| R7 | Gathering life-skills (fishing, foraging, mining, farming) feeding crafting | MERGE | PRG-23 (gathering family listed in PROGRESSION:213), ITM-40..42, WLD-33 (seasonal forage) | Specified; only 12 of the canon skills exist. | M | Part of WP-2E skill roster and 2D tools. Fishing is new as a minigame. Keep it light and deterministic. |
| R8 | Crafting-orders / commission board | MERGE | ITM-61, ITM-79, CMP-80 (ECONOMY systemic contracts) | NPCs and adventurers ordering items is the clearest way to feel the living economy. | M | WP-2D and 2I. The board itself is a quest-board variant (QST-44 bounty board exists). |
| R9 | Map annotations / pins and a discovery atlas | NEW (pins) / MERGE (atlas) | The atlas is specified (WLD-63..70 cartography). Player pins and notes are not. | Players need to remember where things are, especially without quest markers everywhere. | S | Player notes are personal knowledge, so they fit the no-omniscience map. Pins are saved per character. |
| R10 | Photo mode | NEW | Not anywhere | Ultra-first visuals and grand landmarks are made for screenshots. It also helps the owner's visual review. | S | MMO rules: the world keeps running (no pause). The camera detaches and the HUD hides. No AI labels. |
| R11 | Controller support and accessibility (subtitles, colour-blind modes, remapping, UI scale) | MERGE | Accessibility is mostly DONE (CMB-135/136, SYS-31/40, CAN-303/608). Gamepad deferred (CAN-305: "before any paid release"). | Already covered. | — | Remaining accessibility is in WP-1E. Gamepad stays deferred by ruling. |
| R12 | NPC rivals / nemeses | MERGE | CMP-122, CMP-33 (SOCIAL §33; MODDING §44) | Specified, MISSING | M | WP-3E or 3F. Adventurer rivals who race you for bounties fit the NPC-adventurer rule. |
| R13 | Seasonal calendar events | MERGE | WLD-58, WLD-89 (WEATHER doc festivals) | Specified, MISSING | M | WP-1D then 1H. Use the canon calendar (13 tallies × 14 days + Hollow Day). Hollow Day is the natural big festival. |
| R14 | Trade caravans and auction-style market | MERGE caravans / CUT auction | ITM-75 (caravans). ECONOMY:181 says "Do not assume a global auction house". | Caravans move goods; a global auction house conflicts with the doc. | M | Keep local markets and caravans. Possibly a per-town consignment broker that stays local and takes travel time. |
| R15 | Dungeon / instance difficulty by encounter scaling (no presets) | MERGE | CMB-33, CMP-59 (DONE: scaling to party), PRG-15 (no global scaling). The Ring Custodian is fixed power. | Already the design. | — | Presets are banned (MMO rules). Optional "hardened" variants should be world state (a more dangerous occupant arrives), never a menu setting. |
| R16 | Codex of tutorials / hints | MERGE | SYS-166 Field Guide (MISSING), hint system DONE | Specified as the Field Guide | S | WP-1I |
| R17 | **Local bug-report and session journal** | NEW | SYS WP-7 mentions a local bug bundle, not designed | Testers and the owner need a one-key "what just happened" capture: save, log and the last 60 s of state. | S | Local only; never uploaded (no-internet rule). |
| R18 | **Away digest / "while you were gone" letter** | MERGE | RCD6_AWAY_DIGEST exists (CMP rows) | Offline time always passes. Players need a readable account of what changed. | S | Feed it from the Director history (1H), so world events show up in it. |
| R19 | **Player-run shop / stall** | NEW | Not specified (ECONOMY covers NPC traders only) | A sink and a reason to craft in bulk. Adventurers can be the buyers. | M | Needs housing (1G) and economy breadth (2I). Sales happen while offline, which fits "world lives on". |
| R20 | **Fame and renown visible to NPCs** | MERGE | CMP social fame rows (deferred with the social engine) | NPCs reacting to deeds is what makes an MMO-style world feel shared. | M | Depends on the owner's call on the social engine (0B). |
| R21 | **Companion and pet loss rules (injury, recovery, death)** | NEW (as a rule set) | Partly in companion and pet docs; no single rule | Death stakes must be clear when there is no difficulty setting. | S | Decide once: recoverable injury by default, permanent loss only by story. |
| R22 | **Owner/dev cheat console for testing** | NEW | Harness flags exist; no in-game console | Lets the owner jump to any system state for playtests. | S | Compiled out of release builds; never shown to players. |

## Contradictions found

### Combat, death, stealth, magic, controls

- M3C_STATUS Decision 7 ("one attack per weapon") vs code: TechniqueCommand and three active techniques exist (D11). Code wins; OD-C22 says PT supersedes Decision 7 fully.
- M3_STATUS "Not done: input remapping, accessibility beyond FOV and sensitivity" vs code: full remap, hold/toggle, UI scale, colour-vision, subtitles, comfort presets exist (CMB-135, CMB-136). Code wins; doc stale.
- M3_STATUS camera table "first person hides only the head" vs MetaHumanFigure set_shadow_only hiding the whole figure (CMB-143). Code differs from doc and from CAMERA:142-153; unresolved.
- CAMERA:276-282 (no forced cinematic dialogue camera) vs TalkCamera (CMB-146). Code is built; owner has not ruled it against the doc.
- HUD:24-36 lists radial menus vs content bible section 9 "no radial menu" (Main.Input.cs:389) vs PLAN:842 proposing a party radial. Undecided; code has none.
- STEALTH:185-235 and CAP05 OD-C22(d) (armour/anatomy decide, no one-shot immunity flags, level never grants immunity) vs code: TakedownRules.Executes kills through armour (Execution sets final damage to max health), is gated on Threat <= Even (a level-gap read) and excludes the "boss" tag or any recovery-site creature (CMB-84, CMB-85). The owner finding 26 / RC d5 instruction (CODEX_PROGRESS:2044: "one-shot sneak kills... resistance belongs only to aware, higher-level or boss targets") wins in game; it conflicts with COMBAT:522-526 and STEALTH:220-235 and with spec 25-27's own risk note.
- SPEC25-27 and SPEC24 and SPEC29 ("proposal, not started"; "no dagger, no sleep/paralyse/root, nothing consumes light_radius/stealth_penalty, no threat colour system") vs code: all built (CMB-84, 92, 94, 95, 97, 98, 48). Specs stale; code wins.
- SPEC24 "show a band word, not the exact level" vs TargetPlate showing "Level N" plus the band word (DS flagged the deviation; PROGRESSION keeps numbers soft). Open owner call.
- M3D_STATUS Reconciliation 4 ("bible's Downed/Dying/Dead replaces M3c death... until the layout brings the Waystone") vs code: bound-stone return and companion-gated Downed are built (CMB-51, CMB-52); Dying/Critical are not. Doc stale.
- CAP08 / canon OWNER_RULINGS_2026-09-26 say OD-11 and OD-13 "stay open" vs PLAN:348,541 (OD-13 = B built; OD-11 closed "standard rules only", 2026-10-17). Later ruling wins; its original text is not in OWNER_RULINGS_2026-10-05_to_14.txt (it covers to 10-14) and is recorded only in PLAN.
- VERTICAL_SLICE P8 (death = XP debt + temporary injury + corpse-recovery trip, via CAP08 1.3) and PJ10 3.5 World Rules vs PLAN:348 (XP debt is the one death consequence). Owner ruling wins.
- COMBAT:414-420 injury states vs code: only Healthy, Downed (with a hand), Dead. Wounded, Critical, Dying absent (owner OD-13 = B).
- HUD:65-81 "changing full armour sets restricted in combat" vs EquipCommand allowing instant armour swaps in a fight (CMB-28); gear-set switching complies.
- M3C_STATUS "Creature state is not saved until M3d" and "Not done: creature perception" vs M3D_STATUS and code (saved creature records, perception). Stale, harmless.
- M3C audit L-25 (swing cadence one tick long) documented but not ruled; M3C_TTK numbers are generated from that behaviour.

### Progression, races, creation, endgame

- Skill decay: docs/PROGRESSION.md:227 says 0.5 points/world-week below 60; OD-C02 sets decay to 0 at 1.0 (rust post-1.0). Owner ruling wins; PROGRESSION.md is stale.
- Skill list: PROGRESSION.md:208-215 and SKILLS_AND_DISCIPLINES.md list 60+ names (armour, riding, lockpicking, pickpocket, logging, fishing, lore...); canon 03 + OD-C17(d) cap 1.0 at 43 skills and fold those; content has 12 and names `handling` (canon `animal_handling`) and keeps `salvage` (folded by OD-C17(d)). Owner ruling wins.
- Race learning statements: RACES.md:149 ("fastest skill acquisition, lowest skill cap") and :256 ("highest ceiling, slowest learning") vs PROGRESSION.md §4.1/§11.3 (only a bounded creation grant), PJ OD-08 (open) and OD-C23 (never racial XP multipliers or ceilings). OD-C23 wins; treat as tendencies. PROGRESSION.md:297 ("small non-dominant modifier package") also conflicts with canon "people differ by body, not flat bonuses".
- Archetypes: PROGRESSION.md:297 names warrior/mage/rogue/ranger/healer/artisan; VERTICAL_SLICE names Blade/Hunter/Ember Adept/Warden; content and OD-C03 use Calls (Wayfarer, Scholar, Smith's hand, Hedge healer). OD-C03 wins. PROGRESSION.md:289 also lists "a small starting technique package"; OD-C03 accepts zero techniques before PT.
- Call contents vs canon C11 proposals: scholar force+warding (canon force+research), hedge healer vital+survival (canon vital+medicine), smith's hand smithing+polearm (canon smithing+survival), wayfarer bias endurance 2/might 1/agility 1 (canon might/endurance/will/agility 1 each). Content notes say provisional pending C11 text; content stands until skills research/medicine exist.
- Magic potency: PROGRESSION.md:365 "scaled by will + insight + domain skill" vs code (Resonance only; Insight unwired). Code stands (canon 02:§6.7); Insight composition ceiling is proposed (canon 03:§2.9).
- Threat reads: PROGRESSION.md:162 ("lethal/severe/even/trivial", never a numeric level, knowledge-bound) vs TargetPlate.cs "Level N" plus six words (owner finding 24). Code stands pending a ruling; flag for owner.
- Characters pipeline: RCD6_CHARACTER_RELEASE.md ("characters.option chooses production MetaHumans or MPFB") and CANONICAL_BODY_AND_SKELETON.md (24/52-bone fit-family bodies) vs owner ruling "HumGen is the only pipeline" (59 native bones, RC_D9). Ruling wins.
- Stature/fit: CANONICAL_BODY (Kal 1.30 m compact_broad, Ondrek 2.60) vs RACES.md:565-578 owner-approved 2026-10-23 (Kal 1.50-1.75, Ondrek 2.00-2.60); humgen_creator.json gives Siann fit_family compact_broad while CANONICAL_BODY and ARMOUR_TIERS_DESIGN put Siann in standard_humanoid. Owner-approved table wins; catalogue fit family for Siann looks wrong.
- Heritage peoples order: HERITAGE_DESIGN.md:7 order Kal, Siann, Orenth after Veth vs canon OD-C17 target list Veth, Siann, Orenth, Kal. Owner's 2026-10-24 statement wins.
- Heritage vs FD-4: HERITAGE_DESIGN refuses "use" of a heritage item by other peoples ("means nothing in your hands"); canon FD-4/UI-6 forbid "<people> only" restrictions. Owner ruling (heritage items are identity, 2026-10-24) wins; words must be phrased as world fact.
- Endgame gating: ENDGAME_QUESTLINES.md:41-72 (native/adopted/borrowed/rented) vs OD-C13 notes that splitting native access (TR-2) is "consistent but proposed"; and the eight chains include peoples that OD-C17 puts at stretch/post-1.0. Content for those peoples is deferred; the model stays proposed.
- Prestige: Charter §24 lists "prestige systems"; PROGRESSION.md:575 rejects reincarnation prestige. Charter wins in principle but D-09 amendment reads it as experiential; no code conflict.
- Death penalty variants (reviews/PROGRESSION_SCOPE_ECONOMY_REVIEW.md:127-140) are resolved: AG-8 XP debt is canonical and implemented.
- CHARACTER_CREATION_AND_LINEAGE.md:66-81 treats Background as separate from the starting archetype; PJ 05 merges Background into the Call until Upbringing exists. PJ 05 + OD-C13 win.

### Items, crafting, economy, inventory

- **Bar size and storage**: C26 §8.2/§8.6 says eight slots, never growing, stored outside the save in client prefs. ABGS (owner ruling 2026-10-18) says six bars of twelve, layout saved with the character (schema 32), only visibility/placement in `InterfacePrefs`. Owner ruling wins; code follows it (ClientPrefs.cs, QuickBar.cs).
- **Pack schema number**: INVORG:27 says schema 33, ABGS:20 says 32 then 34; `SaveFormat.SchemaVersion` = 39 (src/Persistence/SaveModel.cs:21). Docs are stale; code and migrations win.
- **RCD6 doc vs code**: RCD6:13 says "#46 remains partial until salvage and the first-full-bag hint are implemented"; both exist (Salvage.cs, SalvageTests, Main.Guidance.cs:126). Code wins; RCD6 is stale.
- **Paper doll pipeline**: RCD6:5 says the doll reuses the "native MH/MPFB figure factory"; owner ruling 2026-10-23 makes HumGen the only character pipeline. Ruling wins; the doll must be re-pointed (ITM-107).
- **Worn armour state**: HWAP:3 says "no armour fitting, runtime attachment code or coverage shader is implemented" while `art_bindings.json` + `WornOutfit.cs` + WornOutfitTests implement worn armour for MetaHuman Veth bodies (jack, coif, sabatons, cap, boots, gloves). Both true: implemented for the stopped MetaHuman route, absent for HumGen. HumGen is the ruling, so the item is MISSING (ITM-25).
- **Per-lame repair**: ATD §4.3 lists lame-by-lame repair as a playtest must-show; lamework_jack.yaml says "per-lame repair deferred". Content note, no owner ruling found; treated as PARTIAL, not DEFERRED-BY-OWNER.
- **Heritage items**: HERITAGE_ITEMS_PROPOSAL flags items `no_sell`/`no_drop`; HERITAGE_DESIGN.md (owner, 2026-10-24) replaces `no_sell` with the within-people trade rule and amends drop to "never dropped". HERITAGE_DESIGN wins; none of it is in HEAD (branch claude/heritage-veth).
- **Torch**: Owner ruling (CODEX_PROGRESS.md:1978) says the torch is discovered and crafted, not known at start; ItemizationTests `TheForge_TeachesTheTorch…` and recipe.smithing.torch agree. config.inventory starting_items has no torch. No conflict.
- **Crude cap default**: C12 §9.9 notes code default `CrudePercentMax` 50 vs content 40; content/config/crafting.yaml now says 40 and `CraftingConstants` default is still 50 (src/Domain/Crafting/Crafting.cs:80). Content wins in play; default is a trap for tests.
- **Vaskaal enchanted gear**: C12 OD-CAND-12-2 leaves open whether wielder-sourced enchantments work for a Vaskaal; S47 says nothing. Open owner question, nothing to build until enchanting exists.
- **Salvage vs spec 47**: S47:37-41 and C12 §6.3 expect salvage to recover enhancements and essences; `SalvageRules.Recover` yields recipe materials only (ITM-69).
- **Item "movement_penalty"**: DATA_MODEL/ATD list it; lint accepts it; nothing reads it (ITM-20).

### Companions, NPC life, social

- Party size: COMP:12-18 and PLAN DN11 say player + 3; S43 ruling 2 says start at 4 followers; config/party.yaml max_members 4 and PartyTests.ThePartyHoldsFour pin 4. Winner: S43 owner ruling (later, 2026-10-21). Docs COMPANIONS/COMPANION_MATRIX index (docs/INDEX.md:88 "player + 3 later") are stale.
- Pet death: PETS:157 ("not immortal by default") and S43:193 (permanent) vs S43 ruling 1 (not permanent). Winner: ruling 1. Hirelings still die permanently (DN9), so "pets never die, hirelings do" is intentional.
- PETS doc §13 says focus-weakest / flank / call-for-help behaviours are "not built"; content/config/creature_behaviour.yaml and EnemyTacticsTests show they are. Doc is stale.
- Companions.cs:122 comment says companion kills earn the character no XP; owner ruling 2026-10-24 (CP:3390) and CreatureSystem.Die give full credit. Ruling wins, comment stale.
- COMP:201-203 and SOC:449-463 want NPC/NPC relationships and social life; owner ruling 2026-10-17 (PLAN §2.13) cut pairs, visits, needs. Ruling wins.
- SOC header defers "all deeper social systems" (2026-09-23) while the 2026-10-24 "systems-complete first" goal covers all systems. Needs one owner confirmation whether CMP-103..131 are in scope.
- AINS:57,139-143 allows OpenAI-compatible and LAN endpoints; NoNetworkTests (owner rule 2026-10-17) forbids any networking type in src. The no-network rule wins; AINS should be rewritten to local-in-process only or archived.
- DEEPSEEK_PROJECT_CHATTER_CORPUS.md:139 says the game discloses AI-written chat at channel level; owner ruling 2026-10-21 removed all in-play AI labels. Ruling wins; the Credits disclosure remains by explicit later ruling (CP:2067) and Acceptance 18.1 still checks it.
- REPORT_FOR_CLAUDE_CHATTER.md:291-295: templates carry ai_generated: false although a model drafted them (lint CHT011 forces it); provenance is recorded only in the file notes.
- COMPANION_MATRIX.md repeats the "Constructed row is all yes" paragraph (lines 50-55 and 89-94), and its reach table (lines 78-87) does not obviously follow from the willingness table (e.g. Siann column has no outright refusals except Mor, yet Siann is listed as shut out of Ondrek and Vaskaal). Re-derive before building CMP-37.
- S15 states "Only 8 authored named NPCs"; the RCD6 inn doc and DS report move Renn into the inn and the adventurers' count to 3 in the hamlet (config target: 3), while S15 open question 1 is still unanswered in the doc; code follows the DS dial.
- Spec 43 lists boar and hawk as tameable; content has them tameable: false (ember hounds and bosses are the only intended non-tameables).

### Factions, quests, lore, dungeons

- CRIME doc and CR:SYSTEMS.md S-27 present crime, bounties and pardon as part of factions; ROADMAP.md:282 puts them in M7. M7 design (approved Q2, 2026-09-25) defers all crime/bounty/pardon/territory gating, PROTOTYPE/INDEX say Phase 3, BIBLE:1083 lists full crime/court simulation as a non-goal. Code follows the deferral. Winner: the Q2 ruling (deferred), unless the owner's 2026-10-17 "complete in every aspect other than content" bar is read to include crime; the SYSTEMS_COMPLETENESS_PLAN_20261017 inventory (ASSET_REBUILD) never lists crime, so this is ambiguous and needs an owner call.
- The word "bounty" means two things: CRIME:144-159 (law-enforcement contracts on wanted people) vs shipped content/quests/bounty/ and QuestTrack.Bounty (standing monster/materials hunting board). Winner: shipped code for the board; rename or namespace the legal kind when built (suggest "warrant contract").
- QDW:97-113 says avoid identical timer respawns; code and content use `respawn: kind timer` (CombatContent.cs:437) and AG-3 doubling. Winner: code for now (doc is direction); noted as QST-90.
- ROADMAP.md:260-263 says Phase 1 refuses factions, bosses, companions objectives; code now builds faction_reputation, defeat_boss, companion_present, dialogue_choice. Roadmap text is stale; code wins.

- docs/LORE_SPEC41_STATE.md:41 says "20 in the index, 6 remaining questions"; repo LORE_OWNER_QUESTIONS.md has 5 open rows; EXT:LORE_OWNER_QUESTIONS.md has 24 open rows plus answered FR-1..FR-5. EXT copy is newest; the repo copy lacks heritage H-1..H-7, opening O-1..O-4, GS-1..GS-10 and FR rows.
- FIELD_RUINS_PROPOSAL (32 m spire) vs FIELD_RUINS_CANON_FOR_OWNER (nearly 100 m Needle, 8 m warden, violet glow) after owner "BIG things" direction 2026-10-24; later doc wins. Neither is in the audited tree (branch claude/field-ruins).

The owner's "complete systems" bar (2026-10-24, systems-complete first) would pull these in; there is no explicit deferral ruling, so they are MISSING not DEFERRED.

### World, travel, maps, housing, MP, performance

- Waystone world-time: canon 19 §9.3 and OD-C16 say a quarter of the walk passes; DN8 (2026-10-17) says travel passes none. Code and TravelTests follow DN8, but content/config/travel.yaml:8, TravelRules/Journeys.Cost (WorldTicks) and Domain TravelRulesTests.AJourney_LetsAShareOfTheWalkPassInTheWorld still encode the quarter. DN8 wins; remove the dead data.

Inn spec 15 was accepted by Claude later (CODEX_PROGRESS.md:2220); needs an explicit owner statement that the inn is the one exception.
- WORLD_ARCHITECTURE "Phase 0, no implementation exists" and D-12 "no MMO infrastructure" vs owner 2026-10-17 (world lives on, NPC adventurers, Otherservers/MMO-ready) and DN1/DN2: offline cap 30 game-days (doc :248) vs 183 days (content/config/time.yaml); "abstract tiers never resolve death/skill/coin" (doc :275-288,385) vs DN2. Rulings win; the doc is stale.
- WORLD_ARCHITECTURE §5.5 spawn budgets/night_bias/drift vs CombatContent.cs:408 "Phase 1: a fixed count": doc describes a system the code does not have (WLD-113).
- WEATHER doc (weather and survival change gameplay, slice includes temperature/wetness/camping/hazard) vs D6 "no survival effects" and the not-the-Sims trim: ruling wins for now; the doc's vertical-slice staging (:700-712) is unmet and undecided.
- SKY_WP7_GAP.md (weather applied once at Ready; WP7 rain/fog/wind have no profile; particle emitters fixed at build) vs code: Main.cs:1372 follows the sim every frame, Art/weather_states.json has rain/fog/wind/storm, AtmosphereView decides emitters each frame. Doc is stale except: no tick-based front blend, no calendar-driven sun/moon (still true).
- FRONTIER_PERFORMANCE.md "WP7 sky/weather excluded from perf/locked" and 60 FPS gate vs this branch (includes WP7 sky) and the owner's waiver; REPORT_FOR_CLAUDE_WORLD_PERF "game paced to tick rate" vs SettingsStore.cs:223 (gameplay uses the cap; only capture branches pace to the tick).
- Ultra-first ruling ("default for the owner's builds is Ultra", CODEX_PROGRESS.md:2444) vs VisualOptions.DefaultTier = "high" and tests/Presentation.Tests/PlaytestLaunchDefaultsTests.cs pinning `tier=high`; RenderTiers.cs:25-27 comment still says Ultra moves tree crowns to an impostor at 225 m, but ArtLibrary.IsTree draws trees full detail at every distance. Code behaviour for trees follows the ruling; default tier and comment do not.
- MAPS doc §13 row 6 "a tracked quest shows its own marker" vs MapPanel.cs:15 "no quest pin": the marker is on the compass (Quests.cs Marker), not the map. Consistent once read as compass.
- Named calendar (restore rulings :886 "9th of Roadmend", proposed tally names) vs owner 2026-10-17 numbered tallies (Calendar.cs, CalendarWords). Numbered wins.
- TRAVEL doc "Otherways are places, not menu buttons" vs the built WaystonePanel destination list: accepted (stones are infrastructure, OD-C16); only Otherways must stay physical.
- ENGINE_VALIDATION Phase 1 gate (1080p/60 on RAZER's 4070 Ti) vs owner 2026-10-06 "do not trim to a 4070 Ti" and 2026-10-18 waiver. Later rulings win.

### UI, audio, save, onboarding, owner findings

- **Pause**: PJ03 4.5/6.5, PJ09 7, PJ10 7 and accepted OD-12 say the System Menu pauses; UIUX brief (2026-10-02), SYSTEMS plan D1 (2026-10-17) and MMO rules say nothing pauses anywhere. Ruling wins; code obeys it and `NoPauseDoctrineTests` enforces it.
- **Difficulty / World Rules**: PJ03 5 and OD-11 propose presets; owner ruling D10 closes OD-11 as standard rules only. Ruling wins.
- **Quit / manual saves / quickload**: PJ10 7 wants "Save before quitting?", Quit to main menu, manual save UI, F9 quickload. DN7 (one live state, F5 save-now, no quickload, always save on quit) wins; code has no F9. PERSISTENCE 8.2 "unlimited manual saves" is still true in the engine but has no UI.
- **Offline cap**: WORLD_ARCHITECTURE example "30 game days" vs DN1 183 days (one Reckoning). `config/time.yaml` follows DN1. PERSISTENCE 5.5 (persisted honoured amount) is consistent with both.
- **While-you-were-away report**: DECISIONS default "no report in Phase 1-2" vs OD-18 closed with a summary (2026-10-17). Summary wins; built as the away sheet.
- **AI labels**: DN12 (SYSTEMS plan) discloses AI chat at channel level in Settings and chat header; owner ruling 2026-10-21 removes all in-play AI labels, keeps Credits AI-use section. RELEASE_NOTES_DRAFT and checklist 17-18 are consistent with the ruling (disclosure in Credits and notes only).
- **Persistence layout**: PERSISTENCE 3.2 lists `journal.jsonl`, `command_log.jsonl`, `orphans.msgpack`, `preview.png`, `companions.msgpack` and `saves/<profile>/<slot>`; code writes none of the first four, keeps companions in player.msgpack, and uses `characters/<id>/` (OD-14). Code wins; the doc was never amended (PJ10 5.2 RC-01).
- **Repository structure**: ARCHITECTURE 3 shows two test projects and three layers; the repo has ten test projects plus World, Persistence, EntityRegistry, SaveTool assemblies. Code wins; doc stale. DATA_MODEL 1 kind table (21 kinds) vs ~30 content directories. Doc stale.
- **Migration status docs**: M2B_STATUS says the chain is 1->2->3 and 240 tests; code is schema 39 with ~38 steps and fixtures v1-v39. PERSISTENCE 6.2 table lists only part of the chain. BEFORE_TESTERS says schema 34. All historic; code wins.
- **Scoped saves / autosave triggers**: PERSISTENCE 8.1 makes scoped saves unconditional; PJ10 4.2 adds first-frame, boundary, close, combat-deferral triggers. Code has timer + quest-complete + stone travel only (10 s spacing, not 30 s).
- **Audio source policy**: AUDIO_PIPELINE/AUDIO_DESIGN say every score and sound is generated original and not licensed from libraries; the live SFX are recorded-library layers (Cosmos, Sonniss, Kenney) and the interim music is Stable Audio Open. Owner accepted both; docs are stale. (No licence question raised: settled.)
- **Audio format/naming**: AUDIO_DESIGN names `<category>_<subject>_<variant>.ogg` Vorbis/Opus; manifest ids are dotted and SFX are 24-bit WAV; music Vorbis. Code wins.
- **Audio set size**: V3 handoff 230 files (`v3_delivered`) vs shipped manifest 388 rows (recorded_world 281 + recorded_creatures 107) vs AUDIO_RECOVERY 478 vs RC d9 install 481 rows. EVENT_CONTRACT 10 "no music planned" is stale.
- **Music rule**: AUDIO_DESIGN originally "second visit" and "set-piece combat only"; code and the 2026-10-24 ruling play combat music in every fight and add the tension layer and road-in exception. AUDIO_DESIGN.md now carries the ruling; OPENING_AND_MUSIC_PROPOSAL 0 describes the pre-ruling disagreement.
- **UI scale range**: RELEASE_NOTES_DRAFT "0.75 to 1.5"; checklist 15 and code allow 0.75-2.0. Code wins.
- **Barrowworks**: RELEASE_NOTES_DRAFT "behind a launch option" vs MORNING_SUMMARY (being set default ON). Pending the Barrowworks session.
- **Creator**: checklist 1.2 "no separate Face step (D3)" vs MORNING_SUMMARY 8-step HumGen creator. Checklist stale.
- **Paper doll**: SYSTEMS plan D12 "keep the 5x2 grid" vs owner finding 46 (build it) and `InventoryPanel.PaperDoll.cs`. Owner finding wins; "equipped items leave the bag" part is not done.
- **UI recovery doc**: UI_GAP_AND_RECOVERY (2026-10-17) lists Settings, Skills, Reputation, rebinding, subtitles, UI scale as missing; all are built now. The doc is historic.
- **Release gating**: PJ02 6 wants harness flags off in release; `Main.cs` still parses them (only dev keys are gated).
- **Hints**: PJ07 3.1 pacing budget vs code (queue of 3, no rate limit).

- Wave-1 00:9 says the level-50 cap lands at 65-85 h; wt config/level_cap.yaml has level_cap_phase1: 5 in force (prototype). Config choice, not a doc conflict.
- Atlas PRECEDENCE.md:19 notes Wave-1 03:165 inverts who fears whom (Vaskaal vs Mor); the mythology matrix wins (content only).
- ENDGAME_QUESTLINES: canon (with owner ruling 2026-10-06, lines 10-19, chain endings softened to "strongest explanation") vs docs/ENDGAME_QUESTLINES.md (pre-ruling, states "the answer", e.g. Mor culprit, author in the text). Owner ruling wins: replace the docs copy with the canon copy. GAP_LIST.md and outline 1.2/3.8 still list this as open "ENDGAME-MYTH"; it is ruled.
- Party size: canon COMPANIONS...md:14 and PROGRESSION s11.2 say player + up to 3; code content/config/party.yaml has max_members 4 "others" (owner pets ruling 2, CODEX_PROGRESS.md line 2311, pets docs s13 "now 4"). Owner ruling wins; COMPANIONS doc in docs/ not updated.
- Pets: canon PETS...md:157 "do not make every pet immortal by default" vs owner ruling 1 (pets never die, go home wounded). Ruling wins (docs s13).
- Downed: COMBAT...md:414-441 describes Downed and Dying for everyone; code gives Downed only when an ally is near (OD-13 = B, damage_constants.yaml:26). Owner decision wins.
- Pause: PHASE1 BIBLE:667-668 "optional pause or slow-time in single-player" vs no-pause ruling 2026-10-17 (NoPauseDoctrineTests). Ruling wins.
- Audio: canon AUDIO_DESIGN.md:168-175 (combat music only in set pieces) vs docs copy (every fight plus tension layer, owner 2026-10-24). Docs wins; code agrees.
- Website vs code: playtest page and devlog promise F9 quickload; code deliberately has none (Main.Saves.cs:80-83). Published wins unless the site is corrected: flag (CAN-716).
- Website says "build" (index.astro:20) while building is a stub: flag (CAN-717).
- HUD: PHASE1 BIBLE:577-588 sketches an 8-slot hotbar; code has a 12-slot paged bar. Minor; code wins.
- Combat core numbers (outline 6.1: armour/(armour+50), crit 1.5, stagger) are placeholders; no conflict with docs.
- COMPANION_MATRIX.md duplicates the "The Constructed row is all yes" paragraph (lines 50-60 and 89-94) in canon and docs alike: editorial.
- GAME_OUTLINE.md states website calls the Written Pacha "AI-generated"; the current world.astro source comment says that was corrected on 2026-10-06 ("designed and written"), and the Kal / Constructed alt text was fixed. Outline is stale on this point.
- Peoples: RACES.md:207 gives Kal "reduced spell capacity" but "No affinity for magic at all" (CI-02) and Ondrek "No magic" vs "never a prohibition" (CI-01); unresolved by OD-08. Canon-internal.
- Relationship dimension sets: canon lists seven (affection, trust, respect, loyalty, fear, attraction, ideology), code has six (no attraction or ideology) by DN10, deferred with romance.
- Level cap: soft cap 50 in canon vs level_cap_phase1 5 in content/config/level_cap.yaml; a prototype number, not a conflict.
- docs/PROTOTYPE.md:96 and docs/DATA_MODEL.md:788 (and worktree content/skills/athletics.yaml note "wired in M3f") say athletics gives +8% move speed; code and content have neither the passive nor any training site (CAN-710/711). Code wins; docs are stale or the feature regressed.
- tests/Domain.Tests/Progression/QuickBarTests.cs:41 asserts `ability.blade.riposte` is holdable, but the ability does not exist in content (CAN-720); the test only checks the id prefix.
- Worktree content/pieces/*.yaml notes say "WP8 ports the piece itself" while `piece` is a defined kind: pieces are identity-only (STUB) though DATA_MODEL treats them as full definitions.
- Old merchant `requires: min_tier` (CAN-740) disagrees with owner ruling O-04 (standing never decides service). Owner ruling wins; drop CAN-740 unless the owner says otherwise.

## Owner rulings register

Legend for source paths (line numbers are in the file as it stands on disk today):
- **ORR** = `docs/restore/OWNER_RULINGS_2026-10-05_to_14.txt` (worktree copy).
- **OD15** = `C:\Otherreach_Recovery\ASSET_REBUILD\OWNER_DECISIONS_20261015.md` (the full 170-line ledger). The file named in the task, `C:\Otherreach_External_Assets\OWNER_DECISIONS_20261015.md`, is a 33-line later addendum, cited as **OD15-add**.
- **CP** = `C:\Otherreach_External_Assets\CODEX_PROGRESS.md`.
- **CAPOR** = `C:\Otherreach_Recovery\canon_readonly\direction\capability-progression\OWNER_RULINGS_2026-09-26.md`.
- **UD** = `canon_readonly\audit\UNRESOLVED_DECISIONS.md` (the Player Journey 14 rulings of 2026-09-26). **CR** = `canon_readonly\audit\CONTRADICTION_REGISTER.md`. **AUTH** = `canon_readonly\AUTHORITY.md`. **OWNDIR** = `canon_readonly\owner\OWNER_DIRECTION_2026-10-06.md`. **FPT / SPN** = `canon_readonly\owner\FIRST_PLAYTEST_TRANSCRIPT.txt` / `SECOND_PLAYTEST_NOTES.txt`. **HER** = `C:\Otherreach_External_Assets\HERITAGE_DESIGN.md`. **PLAN** = `docs/plans/SYSTEMS_COMPLETENESS_PLAN_20261017.md`.
- "Systems affected" uses SYSTEMS.md ids (S-nn) and the GOV rows below. Precedence (AUTH:54-64): an explicit owner ruling overrides the text it names; a newer ruling beats an older one (the 2026-10-17 MMO rules beat OD-11, OD-12 and OD-18 of 2026-09-26).

## Build-order notes from each audit

### Combat, death, stealth, magic, controls

- WP "Player Techniques core" (CMB-24, 25, 26, 14, 15, 16, 28, 95): owns src/Domain/Combat/*, src/World/Runtime/Combat.cs and Techniques.cs, content/abilities, content/skills, weapon content. Gate: CombatRuleTests, TeachingTests, TtkTableTests and BehaviourMatrixTests regenerated (UNNAMED_WRITE_TTK / UNNAMED_WRITE_MATRIX), ArchitectureTests. Do first: every other combat row (armour gaps, shields, thrown, grapple lite) hangs off CX-01..06. OD-C01 re-baseline of TTK lands here.
- WP "Armour model" (CMB-02, 03, 04, 05, 06, 12, 13): owns armour data in Items.cs and Combat.cs PlayerArmor, content/items/armor; best done with the PT work because gaps need CX-04. Gate: ItemizationTests, CombatRuleTests, DurabilityTests.
- WP "Boss framework" (CMB-38 to 45, then 47): owns a new encounter module beside src/World/Runtime/Creatures.cs, Presentation Ui/TargetPlate.cs and Hud; stand-down (CMB-43) needs a dialogue/skill-check hook in Dialogue.cs. Gate: BarrowworksCustodianTests, BarrowworksRulingsTests plus new encounter tests. Colossals (CMB-46) stay deferred per owner order.
- WP "Stealth depth" (CMB-73, 74, 75, 80 to 83, 88, 90, 93, 121): owns Perception.cs and Creatures.cs perception, plus Factions.cs for the shared witness/information substrate (CMB-82 should be designed once with the crime/law area). Gate: CreatureTests, StealthTests, the Domain StealthTests; add a torch stealth-penalty test (CMB-92).
- WP "Magic grammar and breadth" (CMB-104 to 124): owns src/Domain/Magic, src/World/Runtime/Magic.cs, src/Content/MagicContent.cs (MAG001), a knowledge-record schema step in src/Persistence (new fixture per Fixtures/README), SkillsPanel. Order: targeting (104) then G1 (107), unsafe outcomes (111), implements (112), payment (114), then Necromancy/Enchantment (105), rituals/workings (115, 116), countermagic and evidence (118, 119). Gate: MagicRulesTests, MagicTests, MagicContentTests, persistence fixtures.
- WP "Healing and conditions" (CMB-54 to 58): owns effects content, a medicine skill, condition lint extensions in CombatContent.cs and a healer service; depends on crafting for the alchemy station. Gate: CombatTests, CompanionTests, new condition tests. Do not ship L4 injuries before surgery (OD-C08).
- WP "Defeat, surrender, capture" (CMB-60, 61): must be built with the crime/law and faction areas, not here alone.
- WP "Camera and controls polish" (CMB-137 to 148): src/Presentation/Player/CameraRig.cs, Main.Input.cs, Settings/ClientPrefs.cs, SettingsPanel; small and independent. Needs a presentation test seam for CameraRig to move rows from PARTIAL to DONE. Resolve the radial-menu and dialogue-camera contradictions with the owner first.
- Deferred clusters that should not be scheduled: Soul/incarnation (CMB-67 to 69), grapple depth (CMB-17), Great Works (CMB-117), later domains (CMB-106), peoples-dependent rules (CMB-09, 65, 122), controller parity (CMB-131), colossals (CMB-46), mounts and building camera (CMB-148).
- Content-only fills once the systems above exist: weapons and skills (CMB-08), formulas (CMB-103), creatures (CMB-35).

### Progression, races, creation, endgame

- WP-A Player techniques + latent attributes (PRG-29/30/31/32/39/53/55/20): owns src/Domain/Combat/Techniques.cs, new technique-requirement record in Domain/Progression, Content/CombatContent.cs, ItemContent (weapon `scaling`), World/Runtime/Combat.cs+Techniques.cs, content/abilities. Gates: a new TechniqueRequirementTests, GuardTests untouched, TtkTableTests re-baselined (M3C_TTK_TABLE), TeachingContentTests coverage lint. Unblocks Guard/Hunter/Drifter Calls (PRG-92).
- WP-B Attribute retraining + bounded grants (PRG-56/58): Domain/Progression/ProgressionEngine.cs, Teaching content for attribute trainers, Presentation/Ui/CharacterPanel.cs, a save field for retrain count. Gate: AttributeTests (new Retrain cases), Persistence MigrationTests. Small and independent.
- WP-C Masteries and band content (PRG-24/26/20/21/25): ProgressionRules/CharacterProgression/ProgressionEngine, SkillsPanel, authored mastery challenges; one schema step. Gate: IndependenceTests, ProgressionRecordTests, Persistence round trip. Needs WP-A for capstones.
- WP-D Skill roster pass (PRG-23): content/skills plus re-pointing recipes off skill.survival; mostly CONTENT-ONLY for woodworking, outfitting, alchemy, herbalism, mining; research/medicine/trade need their systems. Gate: ProgressionContentTests, CraftingContentTests.
- WP-E Knowledge, languages, traditions, Upbringing (PRG-37/40/41/78/79/28): Domain/Knowledge, new content kinds (tradition, language), Creator step, a save step. Largest design-to-code package; start with knowledge records (S9) and the creator Upbringing seam. Gate: new KnowledgeRecordTests, CreatorTests, CharacterContentTests.
- WP-F Heritage merge (PRG-73/74/76/80): merge claude/heritage-veth-2 (HeritageRules, trade/use checks, What Was Kept). Gate: HeritageTests, HeritageRulesTests, CharacterContentTests, the full suite baseline. Do this before adding any other people.
- WP-G Playable peoples RX (PRG-59/60/63-66/70/71): people files + body presets + pack motion for Kal, Siann, Orenth, each with a capability-parity review (OD-C23); needs body record/species flags (PRG-67). Owns content/people, content/bodies, humgen_creator.json, World/Runtime (traversal for wings, Step Debt). Gates: CharacterContentTests, CreatorCatalogueTests, per-people new tests.
- WP-H Creator completion (PRG-87/89/90/91/84): saved appearance presets, pose previews, undo/redo, in-world appearance service, pronoun substitution; the bake-per-combination question (PRG-87) is the blocker and is an art-pipeline decision.
- WP-I Social XP wiring and telemetry log (PRG-07/16): Faction/companion/trade events to XpAward(Social); a dev-only Advancement log. Gate: LevelTests, ProgressionBalanceTests.
- Endgame (PRG-99..118) stays behind M10/M12 and the Soul design; the only items buildable earlier are the Stage A seeds (PRG-112) and the access-model plumbing (PRG-116).

Owns src/Content/CraftingContent.cs (or ContentChecks.cs), src/World/Runtime/Trade.cs, src/Domain/Economy/Economy.cs. Gate: tests/Content.Tests/CraftingContentTests.cs, tests/Application.Tests/EconomyTests.cs, tests/Domain.Tests/Economy/EconomyEngineTests.cs, tests/Soak.Tests/EconomyExploitTests.cs. - **WP-ITM-B, gear effects and enhancement (L)**: item equip-effect field (ITM-19), armour mobility/noise (ITM-20), enhancement slots, infusions, bench application, salvage recovery, visuals (ITM-64 to ITM-69), enchanting skill (ITM-67). Owns src/Domain/Items/Items.cs, src/Content/ItemContent.cs, src/Persistence (schema bump and codec), src/World/Runtime/{Items,Crafting,Salvage}.cs, content/skills, content/recipes. Owns src/Domain/Crafting, src/World/Runtime/Crafting.cs, CraftingContent.cs, content/skills and stations. Gate: CraftingTests, CraftingRulesTests, CraftingContentTests, DurabilityTests. - **WP-ITM-D, HumGen worn armour (L, art-led)**: ITM-25, 26, 24, 107. Owns src/Presentation/Art/{WornOutfit,ArtBindings}.cs, art_bindings.json, InventoryPanel paper doll. Gate: WornOutfitTests plus the real-time frame-by-frame gate the owner requires; tests/Presentation.Tests/ArtRecordsTests.cs.
- **WP-ITM-E, heritage merge (M)**: merge claude/heritage-veth (ITM-30, 93), add people files per H-1. Owns src/Domain (HeritageRules), Trade.cs, Items.cs use/equip checks, PartySheets.cs, dialogue conditions. Gate: HeritageTests, HeritageRulesTests from the branch plus the full suite.
- **WP-ITM-F, economy breadth (L)**: price levers, faction tax, caravans, systemic contracts, barter, banks, sinks, coin-stock band (ITM-72 to 90). Owns src/Domain/Economy, src/World/Runtime/{Markets,Merchants,Adventurers}.cs, content/markets. Gate: EconomyEngineTests, EconomyHarnessTests, EconomyExploitTests, T-22 chunk-invariance test.
- **WP-ITM-G, storage and logistics (L)**: depends on property/building area. Home storage, remote view, couriers, mail, vault, special storage (ITM-108 to 114). Owns src/World/Runtime/Pack.cs, a new storage module, persistence section. Gate: PackTests, PackPersistenceTests, MigrationTests.
- **Small cluster (S)**: potion bandolier kind, expedition pack, Bag of Holding content (ITM-95, 96), encumbrance on walk speed (ITM-98), bag access time (ITM-97). Owns Pack.cs, Laden.cs, content/items/bag. Gate: PackRulesTests, DurabilityTests laden cases.
- **Content-only queue**: materials, reagents, armour rungs 4-7, stations, ring/amulet items (ITM-22, 43, 44, 58, 18). No new system; each batch needs ItemizationContentTests/lore coverage plus the Kal-stone rule (ITM-46) kept.
- **Quest-area handoff**: Stone Question questline (ITM-47) belongs to the quests/factions area; this fragment only guarantees Kal stone stays a non-resource.
